  • She is the only one who comes here on time.
  • The house in that she lives is very big.
  • This is the house that he lived.
  • Have you ever spoken to the people whom live next door?
  • Mark the words that meaning you don’t know.
  • She loves cats that is black and white.
  • I have a student who always don’t do his homework.
  • I can’t find the book which I left it on the table.
  • She stayed in the hotel at that I work.
  • All the people that saw the accident was shocked.
  • He was the first who came to me.
  • Tom is the boy whom I want to play.
  • What she told me about the students were not true.
  • A scientist who he discovered a new planet has won the Nobel Prize.
  • He read some books that discusses the man who survived the war.
  • This is a picture of my friends with I went on vacation.
31what代替“先行词+关系代词” ›

共 3 个小节 · 9 条要点 · 18 句原句摘录

关系代词what代替“先行词+关系代词”

  • 关系代词引导定语从句，同时起连词和代词的作用
    • 关系代词前有先行词，先行词决定关系代词(who, which, that)。
  • “先行词+关系代词”= what
    • 同时起连词和代词作用的是关系代词(which)，可以同时代替先行词(the thing)和关系代词(which)的是what。
  • 包含先行词的what引导的从句是名词性从句，起名词的作用
    • what引导的从句在句子中起主宾补的作用。
  • 关系代词前有先行词，先行词决定关系代词(who, which, that)。
  • 同时起连词和代词作用的是关系代词(which)，可以同时代替先行词(the thing)和关系代词(which)的是what。
  • what引导的从句在句子中起主宾补的作用。

通过解题治疗语法17

  • 判断出what引导的名词性从句在句子中作表语
    • This is what you want. 这是你想要的。
  • what引导的名词性从句就是宾语
    • You reap...是“你收割……”的意思，reap后要接宾语。what引导的名词性从句就是宾语。
  • what引导的是名词性从句，作宾语
    • I understand...是“我理解……”的意思，后面要接宾语。
  • do的宾语就是what引导的名词性从句
    • Do today...是“今天做……”的意思。
  • This is what you want. 这是你想要的。
  • You reap...是“你收割……”的意思，reap后要接宾语。what引导的名词性从句就是宾语。
  • I understand...是“我理解……”的意思，后面要接宾语。
  • Do today...是“今天做……”的意思。

提高语法水平的处方

  • 填空题答案：1. that 2. that 3. What 4. what 5. that 6. what
    • The mother gives her children everything __________ they want.
    • I’ve told you all __________ I understand.
    • __________she said made me surprised.
    • Did you hear __________ they said?
    • He is the only boy __________ can solve the problem.
    • I know the thing that you did last summer.
  • 选出what用法不同的一项：②
    • 告诉我们该做什么。(what to do...意思是“该做什么”，有疑问的意思。所以what是疑问词)。
    • 这是我想说的。(可以表述为This is the thing which I want to say.没有疑问的意思，所以what是连接代词)。
    • 他给了那位老人什么东西？(是在提问，所以what是疑问词)。
    • 我问她在公园里做什么了。(有疑问的意思，所以what是疑问词)。
    • 我不知道她昨天买了什么。(这是间接疑问句，也有疑问的意思，所以what是疑问词)。
  • The mother gives her children everything __________ they want.
  • I’ve told you all __________ I understand.
  • __________she said made me surprised.
  • Did you hear __________ they said?
  • He is the only boy __________ can solve the problem.
  • I know the thing that you did last summer.
  • 告诉我们该做什么。(what to do...意思是“该做什么”，有疑问的意思。所以what是疑问词)。
  • 这是我想说的。(可以表述为This is the thing which I want to say.没有疑问的意思，所以what是连接代词)。
  • 他给了那位老人什么东西？(是在提问，所以what是疑问词)。
  • 我问她在公园里做什么了。(有疑问的意思，所以what是疑问词)。
  • 我不知道她昨天买了什么。(这是间接疑问句，也有疑问的意思，所以what是疑问词)。
32限定性定语从句与非限定性定语从句 ›

共 6 个小节 · 16 条要点 · 23 句原句摘录

限定性定语从句和非限定性定语从句的区别

  • 限定性定语从句对主句有影响，造成主句语意不完整。
  • 非限定性定语从句对主句句意无影响。

限定性定语从句和非限定性定语从句的实例分析

  • 第一个例句为限定性定语从句，第二个为非限定定语从句
    • 第一个例句：He has two sons who became doctors.
    • 第二个例句：She has four daughters, who are teachers.
  • 限定性定语从句和非限定性定语从句在翻译时的差异
    • 限定性定语从句：She has a phone which doesn’t work.
    • 非限定性定语从句：She has a phone, which doesn’t work.
  • 第一个例句：He has two sons who became doctors.
  • 第二个例句：She has four daughters, who are teachers.
  • 限定性定语从句：She has a phone which doesn’t work.
  • 非限定性定语从句：She has a phone, which doesn’t work.

关系代词前有无逗号对句子意思的影响

  • 关系代词前无逗号，句子意思为“我爱那个不爱我的女孩。”。
  • 关系代词前有逗号，句子意思为“我不会穿衣服的，不穿衣服会把我和朋友们区别开。”。

关系代词that的替换情况

  • 关系代词that可以代替主格和宾格关系代词which或who。
  • “,关系代词”是非限定性定语从句的标志，可以用“连词+代词”代替，但不能用that代替
    • 所有格关系代词(whose, of which)不能用that代替。
    • “介词+宾格关系代词”中的关系代词不能用that代替。
    • “,关系代词”中的关系代词，不能用that代替。
  • 所有格关系代词(whose, of which)不能用that代替。
  • “介词+宾格关系代词”中的关系代词不能用that代替。
  • “,关系代词”中的关系代词，不能用that代替。

关系代词的省略情况

  • 主格关系代词不能省略
    • 在“主格关系代词+ be +分词”构成中，可以整个省略掉“主格关系代词+be”。
  • 所有格关系代词不能省略
    • 省略后不清楚是谁所有，所以不能省略。
  • 宾格关系代词可以省略
    • 宾格关系代词后一定会出现主语和谓语，从前后文可以判断出宾语是什么，所以为避免重复，可以省略宾格关系代词。
  • 在“主格关系代词+ be +分词”构成中，可以整个省略掉“主格关系代词+be”。
  • 省略后不清楚是谁所有，所以不能省略。
  • 宾格关系代词后一定会出现主语和谓语，从前后文可以判断出宾语是什么，所以为避免重复，可以省略宾格关系代词。

提高语法水平的处方

  • 将由关系代词连接的句子分成两个句子
    • The girl (whom we met last week) will leave for America.
    • The lady (who is reading a book) is my aunt.
  • 根据汉语意思填空
    • He has a horse whose name is Grace.
    • Who, that is the woman standing over there?
    • Look at the building of which the roof is red.
  • 填写恰当的单词，使题目中的句子意思相同
    • This is the house which he was born in.
    • He loved Jane, who didn’t love him.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • Seoul, that is a dynamic city, has a population of twenty million.
    • This is a picture of my friends with I went on vacation.
    • I bought a computer, that is very good.
    • The girl whose eyes be brown is Ann.
  • 回答下列问题
    • 翻译句子：The girl whom we met last week will leave for America.
    • 选出下列画线部分不能省略的一项：① The man whom you met is my teacher. ② Look at the dog which is barking at us. ③ Do you know who is going to meet him? ④ I saw some girls who were playing there. ⑤ This is the book which she bought for me.
  • The girl (whom we met last week) will leave for America.
  • The lady (who is reading a book) is my aunt.
  • He has a horse whose name is Grace.
  • Who, that is the woman standing over there?
  • Look at the building of which the roof is red.
  • This is the house which he was born in.
  • He loved Jane, who didn’t love him.
  • Seoul, that is a dynamic city, has a population of twenty million.
  • This is a picture of my friends with I went on vacation.
  • I bought a computer, that is very good.
  • The girl whose eyes be brown is Ann.
  • 翻译句子：The girl whom we met last week will leave for America.
  • 选出下列画线部分不能省略的一项：① The man whom you met is my teacher. ② Look at the dog which is barking at us. ③ Do you know who is going to meet him? ④ I saw some girls who were playing there. ⑤ This is the book which she bought for me.
33Part 07 定语从句和关系副词 ›

共 2 个小节 · 6 条要点 · 6 句原句摘录

诊断你的语法水平

  • 填写恰当的关系代词或关系副词
    • This is the house where my teacher lives.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • New York is a city which the UN members meet.
  • 选择恰当的单词
    • The place (where / which) I want to visit is beautiful.
    • The place (where / which) I want to go is beautiful.
  • 填空并翻译句子
    • She went to his house, where she couldn’t see him.
    • Wait till Monday, when he will come back.
  • This is the house where my teacher lives.
  • New York is a city which the UN members meet.
  • The place (where / which) I want to visit is beautiful.
  • The place (where / which) I want to go is beautiful.
  • She went to his house, where she couldn’t see him.
  • Wait till Monday, when he will come back.

掌握定语从句和关系副词的概念

  • 关系代词引导修饰先行词的定语从句，同时起连词和代词的作用。
  • 关系副词引导修饰先行词的定语从句，同时起连词和副词的作用。
34关系副词的概念 ›

共 3 个小节 · 5 条要点 · 8 句原句摘录

关系副词的概念和产生

  • 关系副词引导定语从句，修饰前面的先行词。
  • 关系副词可以通过“介词+关系代词”转换而来
    • “介词+关系代词(in which)”可以换成关系副词where。
  • “介词+关系代词(in which)”可以换成关系副词where。

关系副词与关系代词的区别

  • 关系代词在句子中代替名词，关系副词代替的是起修饰作用的副词
    • 关系代词在句子中代替名词，如果去掉关系代词就不是完整的句子。
    • 关系副词代替的是起修饰作用的副词，所以去掉关系副词，后面的句子依然是完整的。
  • 关系副词where起连接两个句子的连词作用，但它不是代替名词，而是代替副词
    • √ where起连接两个句子的连词作用，但它不是代替名词，而是代替副词there，所以，where是同时起连词和副词作用的关系副词。
  • 关系代词在句子中代替名词，如果去掉关系代词就不是完整的句子。
  • 关系副词代替的是起修饰作用的副词，所以去掉关系副词，后面的句子依然是完整的。
  • √ where起连接两个句子的连词作用，但它不是代替名词，而是代替副词there，所以，where是同时起连词和副词作用的关系副词。

that代替关系代词和关系副词

  • that可以代替主格和宾格关系代词，同样that也可以代替关系副词
    • This is theplacewhich I was born in. → 关系代词which。
    • This is theplacethat I was born in. → 关系代词that。
    • This is theplacewhere I was born. → 关系副词which。
    • This is theplacethat I was born. → 关系副词that。
  • This is theplacewhich I was born in. → 关系代词which。
  • This is theplacethat I was born in. → 关系代词that。
  • This is theplacewhere I was born. → 关系副词which。
  • This is theplacethat I was born. → 关系副词that。
35在关系副词中省略先行词 ›

共 5 个小节 · 11 条要点 · 23 句原句摘录

关系副词中省略先行词的情况

  • 关系副词的先行词可以省略
    • This is (theplace)where I was born. (√)。
    • I remember (theday)when he was born. (√)。
    • This is (thereason)why he was late. (√)。
  • 关系副词how不能与先行词the way一起使用
    • This is thewayhow she solved the problem. (×)。
    • This is thewaythat she solved the problem. (√)。
    • This is how she solved the problem. (√)。
    • This is theway she solved the problem. (√)。
  • This is (theplace)where I was born. (√)。
  • I remember (theday)when he was born. (√)。
  • This is (thereason)why he was late. (√)。
  • This is thewayhow she solved the problem. (×)。
  • This is thewaythat she solved the problem. (√)。
  • This is how she solved the problem. (√)。
  • This is theway she solved the problem. (√)。

通过解题治疗语法7

  • 关系副词how不能与先行词the way一起使用，所以答案是the way
    • Can you tell me the way / the way how you make the cake?
  • 这个句子可以拆分成下列两个句子，合成一个句子是：Can you tell me the way which you solved it in? (√) Can you tell me the way in which you solved it? (√) Can you tell me the way how you solved it? (×) Can you tell me the way that you solved it? (√)
    • Can you tell me the way? 能告诉我那个方法吗？
    • You solved it in the way. 你用那个方法解决了它。
  • Can you tell me the way / the way how you make the cake?
  • Can you tell me the way? 能告诉我那个方法吗？
  • You solved it in the way. 你用那个方法解决了它。

通过解题治疗语法8

  • 关系副词引导的句子都是完整的句子
    • 1 This is the house that he once live in.
    • 2 This is the house he once lived in.
    • 3 This is the house in which he once lived.
    • 4 This is the house in that he once lived.
  • 关系副词是“介词+关系代词”，所以答案是把in where改成in which 或where
    • Chicago is the place (in where John was born).
  • 先行词是表示时间的the time，所以应该把where改成when
    • Please tell me the time (where you met her).
  • 1 This is the house that he once live in.
  • 2 This is the house he once lived in.
  • 3 This is the house in which he once lived.
  • 4 This is the house in that he once lived.
  • Chicago is the place (in where John was born).
  • Please tell me the time (where you met her).

go home和go to home的区别

  • home是表示“家，故乡，祖国”的名词，也是表示“在家，在故乡，在祖国”的副词
    • I go home. (√) I go to home. (×)。
    • You must go. (√) You must go home. (√) You must go to home. (×)。
  • 如果home作名词应该是 I go to the home.(定冠词在名词前。)。
  • I go home. (√) I go to home. (×)。
  • You must go. (√) You must go home. (√) You must go to home. (×)。

关系副词的使用

  • 先行词是表示理由的reason，所以答案是why
    • It is the time __________ we must go home.
    • This is the reason __________ he was absent.
  • 这道题中省略了先行词，所以答案有很多种
    • 意思是“这是他见到她的地方”时，答案是where。
    • 意思是“这是他见她的原因”时，答案是why。
    • 意思是“这是他见她的方法”时，答案是how。
  • It is the time __________ we must go home.
  • This is the reason __________ he was absent.
  • 意思是“这是他见到她的地方”时，答案是where。
  • 意思是“这是他见她的原因”时，答案是why。
  • 意思是“这是他见她的方法”时，答案是how。
36关系副词的用法 ›

共 5 个小节 · 7 条要点 · 28 句原句摘录

关系副词的用法

  • 关系副词引导的句子都是限定性定语从句，但若关系副词前有逗号，则引导的是非限定性定语从句
    • I went to (Tokyo where I met the pretty girl). 我去了(见到了那个漂亮女孩的东京)。
    • I went to Tokyo, where I met the pretty girl. 我去了东京，在那里遇到了漂亮的女孩。
    • He went to America (where the war broke out). 他去了(发生战争的)美国。
    • He went to America, where the war broke out. 他去了美国，那里爆发了战争。
  • 逗号加关系副词可以换成“ 连词+副词”
    • He went to America and there the war broke out.
  • I went to (Tokyo where I met the pretty girl). 我去了(见到了那个漂亮女孩的东京)。
  • I went to Tokyo, where I met the pretty girl. 我去了东京，在那里遇到了漂亮的女孩。
  • He went to America (where the war broke out). 他去了(发生战争的)美国。
  • He went to America, where the war broke out. 他去了美国，那里爆发了战争。
  • He went to America and there the war broke out.

通过解题治疗语法9

  • 将逗号加关系副词改“连词+副词”
    • She went to his house, where she couldn’t see him.
    • Wait till Monday, when he will come back.
  • She went to his house, where she couldn’t see him.
  • Wait till Monday, when he will come back.

治疗你的语法水平

  • 用“连词+副词”替代关系副词
    • 她去了他家，但没见到他。
    • 等到星期一，那时他会回来的。
  • 她去了他家，但没见到他。
  • 等到星期一，那时他会回来的。

通过解题治疗语法10

  • 在非限定性定语从句中，可以用“连词+副词”代替关系副词
    • She first went to Paris, where she received some training to help the sick and poor.
    • 用and there替换where。
  • She first went to Paris, where she received some training to help the sick and poor.
  • 用and there替换where。

提高语法水平的处方

  • 选择恰当的答案
    • I can’t forget the day (when / where) she left me.
    • Is there any reason (when / why) she got angry?
    • Please tell me (the way / the way how) you make the cake.
    • This is the house (which / where) they lived ten years ago.
    • This is the house (which / where / in which) they built two years ago.
    • Sunday is the day (which / when / how) we don’t go to school.
    • America is a country (where / which) English is spoken.
    • The place (where / which) I want to visit in Korea is Seoul.
    • The place (where / which) I want to go in Korea is Seoul.
    • I stayed at the hotel (which / where) he recommended.
    • I stayed at the hotel (which / where) he worked.
    • I don’t remember the day (which / that / where) the pianist was born.
    • I stayed at his hotel for a week, (when / which) I received a letter.
    • Can you tell me the way (how / that) you solved it?
    • I don’t like the way (how / in which) she talks to me.
  • 将两个句子合成一个句子
    • Tell me the time. / The concert begins at the time.
    • I don’t like the way. / He talks in the way.
  • I can’t forget the day (when / where) she left me.
  • Is there any reason (when / why) she got angry?
  • Please tell me (the way / the way how) you make the cake.
  • This is the house (which / where) they lived ten years ago.
  • This is the house (which / where / in which) they built two years ago.
  • Sunday is the day (which / when / how) we don’t go to school.
  • America is a country (where / which) English is spoken.
  • The place (where / which) I want to visit in Korea is Seoul.
  • The place (where / which) I want to go in Korea is Seoul.
  • I stayed at the hotel (which / where) he recommended.
  • I stayed at the hotel (which / where) he worked.
  • I don’t remember the day (which / that / where) the pianist was born.
  • I stayed at his hotel for a week, (when / which) I received a letter.
  • Can you tell me the way (how / that) you solved it?
  • I don’t like the way (how / in which) she talks to me.
  • Tell me the time. / The concert begins at the time.
  • I don’t like the way. / He talks in the way.
37Part 08 状语从句 ›

共 3 个小节 · 6 条要点 · 13 句原句摘录

诊断你的语法水平

  • 比较并翻译两个句子
    • I will buy her a car if she marries him.
    • I don’t know if she will marry him.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • If she will come here, I’ll leave.
    • I will meet him when he will arrive here.
    • I will take a walk if it will be fine tomorrow.
    • I wonder whether he goes to the party or not next week.
  • 改写成伴随状语
    • Because it was cold yesterday, the river is frozen.
    • Since she didn’t see the movie, I want to see it with her.
    • Though she is loved by many people, she is not happy now.
  • I will buy her a car if she marries him.
  • I don’t know if she will marry him.
  • If she will come here, I’ll leave.
  • I will meet him when he will arrive here.
  • I will take a walk if it will be fine tomorrow.
  • I wonder whether he goes to the party or not next week.
  • Because it was cold yesterday, the river is frozen.
  • Since she didn’t see the movie, I want to see it with her.
  • Though she is loved by many people, she is not happy now.

掌握状语从句的概念

  • 状语从句是单独存在的，很容易找出主句和从句
    • 名词性从句和定语从句是将两个句子合成一个句子，不容易快速找出主句和从句。
  • 重点是状语从句修饰主句
    • “我睡觉”是主句，“我洗澡后”是从句，从句修饰主句。
  • 名词性从句和定语从句是将两个句子合成一个句子，不容易快速找出主句和从句。
  • “我睡觉”是主句，“我洗澡后”是从句，从句修饰主句。

治疗你的语法水平

  • 状语从句修饰主句
    • 这句话的意思是“我洗完澡后去睡觉了。”。
    • “我洗澡后”的作用只是修饰“我睡觉”。
  • 这句话的意思是“我洗完澡后去睡觉了。”。
  • “我洗澡后”的作用只是修饰“我睡觉”。
38状语从句的种类 ›

共 5 个小节 · 5 条要点 · 2 句原句摘录

状语从句的种类

  • 状语从句指含有主语和谓语的句子，起副词作用，修饰整个句子。

时间状语从句

  • 时间状语从句中要有表示时间的连接词，如when, as, while, after, before, till, as soon as等。

原因状语从句

  • 原因状语从句中要有表示原因的连接词，如because, as, since 等
    • 前面的状语从句只是起修饰作用，所以没有状语从句，句子本身也是成立的。
  • 前面的状语从句只是起修饰作用，所以没有状语从句，句子本身也是成立的。

让步状语从句

  • 让步状语从句中要有表示让步的连接词，如though, although, even though,while等。

条件状语从句

  • 条件状语从句是指表示“如果”等条件或线索的从句，所以要有表示条件的连接词，如if, unless等
    • “如果”是虚拟语气中常用的单词，可以说虚拟语气也是状语从句的一种，但虚拟语气有些复杂，所以后面我们再专门学习。
  • “如果”是虚拟语气中常用的单词，可以说虚拟语气也是状语从句的一种，但虚拟语气有些复杂，所以后面我们再专门学习。
39状语从句的时态 ›

共 7 个小节 · 9 条要点 · 25 句原句摘录

状语从句的时态

  • 条件状语从句和时间状语从句，都是用现在时代替将来时
    • 条件状语从句中要用现在时，不能用将来时。
    • 时间状语从句中要用现在时，不能用将来时。
  • 条件状语从句中要用现在时，不能用将来时。
  • 时间状语从句中要用现在时，不能用将来时。

条件状语从句的时态

  • 条件状语从句中要用现在时，不能用将来时
    • 表示条件的从句是“如果你左转”，含有将来的意思，但条件状语从句不能用将来时，要用现在时。
    • 如果他回来，我会和他结婚。
    • 如果明天下雨，我就不出去。
  • 表示条件的从句是“如果你左转”，含有将来的意思，但条件状语从句不能用将来时，要用现在时。
  • 如果他回来，我会和他结婚。
  • 如果明天下雨，我就不出去。

时间状语从句的时态

  • 时间状语从句中要用现在时，不能用将来时
    • 表示时间的从句说的是将来会发生的事情，所以似乎应该用表示将来的will，但这种是错误的。
    • 她回来时，我会告诉她真相。
    • 你一到达就给我打电话。
  • 表示时间的从句说的是将来会发生的事情，所以似乎应该用表示将来的will，但这种是错误的。
  • 她回来时，我会告诉她真相。
  • 你一到达就给我打电话。

通过解题治疗语法1

  • 找出错误并改正
    • If she will come here, I’ll leave. → If she comes here。
    • I will meet him when he will arrive here. → When he arrives here。
    • I will take a walk if it will be fine tomorrow. → if it is fine tomorrow。
  • If she will come here, I’ll leave. → If she comes here。
  • I will meet him when he will arrive here. → When he arrives here。
  • I will take a walk if it will be fine tomorrow. → if it is fine tomorrow。

治疗你的语法水平

  • 翻译句子并找出错误
    • 如果她来，我就走。
    • 他到这儿时，我会见到他。
    • 如果明天晴天，我会去散步。
  • 如果她来，我就走。
  • 他到这儿时，我会见到他。
  • 如果明天晴天，我会去散步。

通过解题治疗语法2

  • 比较并翻译句子
    • I will buy her a car if she marries him.
    • I don’t know if she will marry him.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • I wonder whether he goes to the party or not next week. → I wonder whether he will go to the party or not next week.
  • I will buy her a car if she marries him.
  • I don’t know if she will marry him.
  • I wonder whether he goes to the party or not next week. → I wonder whether he will go to the party or not next week.

通过解题治疗语法3

  • 判断从句的类型，并翻译句子
    • He asked if his son was there.
    • He would not have done so if his son had been there.
    • Whatever he buys is cheap and good.
    • Whatever he buys, it is cheap and good.
  • 治疗你的语法水平
    • 他问他儿子是否在那里。
    • 如果他儿子在那里，他就不会那么做了。
    • 他买的所有东西都又便宜又好。
    • 无论他买什么，都又便宜又好。
  • He asked if his son was there.
  • He would not have done so if his son had been there.
  • Whatever he buys is cheap and good.
  • Whatever he buys, it is cheap and good.
  • 他问他儿子是否在那里。
  • 如果他儿子在那里，他就不会那么做了。
  • 他买的所有东西都又便宜又好。
  • 无论他买什么，都又便宜又好。
40伴随状语 ›

共 7 个小节 · 10 条要点 · 50 句原句摘录

伴随状语的概念和构成

  • 伴随状语是将状语从句变成简短的伴随状语，用来说明主句的原因或情况
    • 状语从句包含主语和谓语，描述更详细，但是有时反复说主语、谓语很麻烦。
    • 在状语从句中，也有可以省略的成分，以下是省略的规则：通过上下文可以理解意思时，可以省略引导状语从句的连词；状语从句中的主语和主句的主语相同时，可以省略状语从句的主语；在省略连词和主语时，为避免引起歧义，状语从句的谓语要变为分词。
  • 状语从句包含主语和谓语，描述更详细，但是有时反复说主语、谓语很麻烦。
  • 在状语从句中，也有可以省略的成分，以下是省略的规则：通过上下文可以理解意思时，可以省略引导状语从句的连词；状语从句中的主语和主句的主语相同时，可以省略状语从句的主语；在省略连词和主语时，为避免引起歧义，状语从句的谓语要变为分词。

伴随状语的转换规则

  • 伴随状语转换的顺序是去掉连词→去掉主语→动词变形
    • 把状语从句变成伴随状语时，去掉了引导状语从句的连词或主语，如果动词依然保持原来的形态，容易让听者或读者混淆，所以将其谓语变为现在分词的形态。
  • 把状语从句变成伴随状语时，去掉了引导状语从句的连词或主语，如果动词依然保持原来的形态，容易让听者或读者混淆，所以将其谓语变为现在分词的形态。

伴随状语的应用场景

  • 伴随状语用于说明主句的原因或情况
    • 因为我累了，所以很早就睡觉了。
    • 虽然我感到很累，但还是很早就去睡觉了。
    • 如果我感到很累，我会很早就去睡觉了。
    • 因为我感到很累，所以很早就去睡觉了。
  • 因为我累了，所以很早就睡觉了。
  • 虽然我感到很累，但还是很早就去睡觉了。
  • 如果我感到很累，我会很早就去睡觉了。
  • 因为我感到很累，所以很早就去睡觉了。

伴随状语与状语从句的转换难点

  • 把伴随状语变成状语从句，需要考虑已经去掉的连词、主语和动词
    • 当她看到那只狗时，她就跑开了。
    • 因为现在很冷，所以那条河结冰了。
    • 因为她没看过那部电影，所以我想和她看那部电影。
    • 虽然她备受喜爱，但她现在并不幸福。
  • 当她看到那只狗时，她就跑开了。
  • 因为现在很冷，所以那条河结冰了。
  • 因为她没看过那部电影，所以我想和她看那部电影。
  • 虽然她备受喜爱，但她现在并不幸福。

伴随状语的其他注意事项

  • 不省略连词的情况、主句的主语和从句的主语不一致的情况、有否定词的情况、句首有being的情况、状语从句中的事件比主句中的事件发生得早等情况的处理方式
    • 使用伴随状语的目的是更简洁、准确地沟通。所以有时为了明确表达意思，不能省略连词。
    • 状语从句的主语和主句的主语相同时，可以省略从句的主语，但是前后主语不一致时，不能省略从句的主语。
    • 有never, not等否定词时，应该把这些否定词放在句首。
    • 在being + p.p.中，可以省略being。在being + V-ing中，一定要省略being。
    • 状语从句中的事件先发生，主句中的事件后发生，这时状语从句的动词不能变成现在分词形式，而要用having+ p.p.。
  • 使用伴随状语的目的是更简洁、准确地沟通。所以有时为了明确表达意思，不能省略连词。
  • 状语从句的主语和主句的主语相同时，可以省略从句的主语，但是前后主语不一致时，不能省略从句的主语。
  • 有never, not等否定词时，应该把这些否定词放在句首。
  • 在being + p.p.中，可以省略being。在being + V-ing中，一定要省略being。
  • 状语从句中的事件先发生，主句中的事件后发生，这时状语从句的动词不能变成现在分词形式，而要用having+ p.p.。

通过解题治疗语法

  • 选择恰当的答案，通过实际题目练习语法
    • A：How did you spend your evening yesterday? B：The children __________ to a magic show, I had a quiet time reading a book. 1 going 2 were gone 3 have gone 4 having gone。
  • A：How did you spend your evening yesterday? B：The children __________ to a magic show, I had a quiet time reading a book. 1 going 2 were gone 3 have gone 4 having gone。

提高语法水平的处方

  • 选择恰当的答案，通过实际题目练习语法
    • (Though / Because) he was tired, he couldn’t rest. 2. (Though / Because) he was tired, he wanted to rest. 3. (While / Since) he drank coffee, he read the newspaper. 4. When he (comes / will come) back, I will go out. 5. If it (will be / is) fine tomorrow, we will go on a picnic. 6. I don’t know if it (will be / is) fine tomorrow. 7. Please tell me when he (goes / will go) to school. 8. (Feeling / Felt) cold, she turned on the heater. 9. (Knowing not / Not knowing) her address, I couldn’t visit her. 10.(Being / It being) hot, I turned on the air conditioner.。
  • 找出错误并改正，通过实际题目练习语法
    • I will give him the gift if he will finish his homework. will finish → finishes，完整主句，if引导的条件状语从句，从句用现在时代替将来时。
    • I don’t know if he finishes his homework by tomorrow. finished → will finish，主句不完整，“我不知道……”后面要接宾语，if引导的名词性从句作宾语。
    • She is wondering when he comes back. comes → will come，主句不完整，“她担心……”后面要接宾语，这里特殊疑问词when引导的名词性从句作宾语，意思是“他什么时候回来”。
    • I will go there with him when he will come back. will come → comes，主句完整，后面是when引导的时间状语从句，意思是“他回来时”，从句要用现在时代替将来时。
    • Being cold, we stayed home. Being → it being。
    • Having not any money, he borrowed some money from him. Having not → Not having。
    • Disappointing with her reaction, he was sad. Disappointing with her reaction → Being disappointed with her reaction 或disappointed with her reaction，原来的从句是Because he was disappointed with her reaction。
    • Being finished her degree, she started to work for an international company. 句子的意思是“取得学位后，她开始在一家跨国企业工作。”。
    • Having not a friend at all, she felt lonely. Having not → Not having。
    • Listened to the radio, she didn’t hear the doorbell. Listened改为listening。
  • 改写成伴随状语，通过实际题目练习语法
    • When she found her test results, she was surprised. Finding her test results, she was surprised.。
    • As she is tired, she doesn’t want to go to the party. (Being) tired, she doesn’t want to go to the party.。
    • Since she didn’t have any money, she had to walk. Not having any money, she had to walk.。
    • As it rained, I didn’t want to go there. It raining, I didn’t want to go there.。
    • After he finished his work, he went out. Finishing his work, he went out.。
    • While I walked in the street, I met one of my friends. Walking in the street, I met one of my friends.。
    • As he was poor, he could not buy the house. Being poor, he could not buy the house.。
    • Though I live near her house, I have never seen him. Living near her house, I have never seen him.。
    • As I didn’t know what to say, I didn’t say anything. Not knowing what to say, I didn’t say anything.。
    • Because she visited the museum, she doesn’t want to go there again. Having visited the museum, she doesn’t want to go there again.。
  • 将伴随状语改写成状语从句，通过实际题目练习语法
    • Not seeing me, she didn’t say Hello to me. Because she didn’t see me, she didn’t say Hello to me.。
    • Being at home, I usually read books. When I am at home, I usually read books.。
    • Having finished my homework yesterday, I am free now. Because I finished my homework yesterday, I am free now.。
    • Living next door, I seldom see her. Although I live next door, I seldom see her.。
    • It being foggy, I couldn’t drive. As (=Because) it was foggy, I couldn’t drive.。
    • Though written in easy English, it is difficult for children. Though it is written in easy English, it is difficult for children.。
    • Traveling around Ireland, I always stay in youth hostels. When I travel around Ireland, I always stay in youth hostels.。
    • Not hearing the doorbell, she missed the delivery. Because she didn’t hear the doorbell, she missed the delivery.。
    • Having saved a little money, he travelled to Australia. Because he had saved a little money, he travelled to Australia.。
    • We sat in the bus shelter, waiting for the rain to stop. We sat in the bust shelter while we waited for the rain to stop.。
    • Wounded in the legs, she could not walk. Because she was wounded in the legs, she could not walk.。
    • Generally speaking, our country lacks natural resources. If we speak generally, our country lacks natural resources.。
  • (Though / Because) he was tired, he couldn’t rest. 2. (Though / Because) he was tired, he wanted to rest. 3. (While / Since) he drank coffee, he read the newspaper. 4. When he (comes / will come) back, I will go out. 5. If it (will be / is) fine tomorrow, we will go on a picnic. 6. I don’t know if it (will be / is) fine tomorrow. 7. (Knowing not / Not knowing) her address, I couldn’t visit her. 10.(Being / It being) hot, I turned on the air conditioner.。
  • I will give him the gift if he will finish his homework. will finish → finishes，完整主句，if引导的条件状语从句，从句用现在时代替将来时。

  • Being cold, we stayed home. Being → it being。
  • Having not any money, he borrowed some money from him. Having not → Not having。
  • Disappointing with her reaction, he was sad. Disappointing with her reaction → Being disappointed with her reaction 或disappointed with her reaction，原来的从句是Because he was disappointed with her reaction。
  • Being finished her degree, she started to work for an international company. 句子的意思是“取得学位后，她开始在一家跨国企业工作。”。
  • Having not a friend at all, she felt lonely. Having not → Not having。
  • Listened to the radio, she didn’t hear the doorbell. Listened改为listening。
  • When she found her test results, she was surprised. Finding her test results, she was surprised.。
  • As she is tired, she doesn’t want to go to the party. (Being) tired, she doesn’t want to go to the party.。
  • Since she didn’t have any money, she had to walk. Not having any money, she had to walk.。
  • As it rained, I didn’t want to go there. It raining, I didn’t want to go there.。
  • After he finished his work, he went out. Finishing his work, he went out.。
  • While I walked in the street, I met one of my friends. Walking in the street, I met one of my friends.。
  • As he was poor, he could not buy the house. Being poor, he could not buy the house.。
  • Though I live near her house, I have never seen him. Living near her house, I have never seen him.。
  • As I didn’t know what to say, I didn’t say anything. Not knowing what to say, I didn’t say anything.。
  • Because she visited the museum, she doesn’t want to go there again. Having visited the museum, she doesn’t want to go there again.。

41Part 09 虚拟语气 ›

共 2 个小节 · 5 条要点 · 6 句原句摘录

虚拟语气的概念和用法

  • 虚拟语气表示人的主观愿望、猜测、建议或假设等。
  • 虚拟语气由句中的谓语变化来体现，可以用于条件、目的状语从句及宾语从句中
    • 常见的虚拟语气一般由if引导。
  • 常见的虚拟语气一般由if引导。

虚拟语气的诊断和治疗

  • 通过选择恰当的动词和找出错误并改正来诊断语法水平
    • 例如：If you take the subway, you (will be / would be / would have been) on time.
    • 例如：It’s cold outside. If I am you, I would put a coat on.
  • 通过将句子改成虚拟语气和治疗来掌握虚拟语气的用法
    • 例如：As I don’t have money, I can’t buy you a car.
    • 例如：If I had money, I could buy you a car.
  • 通过选择恰当的答案填空来巩固虚拟语气的用法
    • 例如：___________, I’d have told you. ③ Had I known it。
  • 例如：If you take the subway, you (will be / would be / would have been) on time.
  • 例如：It’s cold outside. If I am you, I would put a coat on.
  • 例如：As I don’t have money, I can’t buy you a car.
  • 例如：If I had money, I could buy you a car.
  • 例如：___________, I’d have told you. ③ Had I known it。
42虚拟语气（if 从句） ›

共 6 个小节 · 17 条要点 · 60 句原句摘录

虚拟语气过去时

  • 虚拟语气过去时表达对现在的希望，也就是表达现在无法实现的事情
    • 如果我是你，我会和他结婚。
    • 如果我英语好，我会帮你做作业。
  • 虚拟语气过去时的表达方法：谓语应该用过去式，主句要有助动词，根据从句的时态，助动词也要用过去式
    • If I knew her number, I would call her.
    • If I had money, I could buy the car.
    • If I were rich, I might go there.
  • 翻译时要注意把从句中的过去时，翻译成现在时
    • If I knew her number, I wouldcall her.
    • If I had money, I couldbuy the car.
    • If I were rich, I couldgo there.
  • 如果我是你，我会和他结婚。
  • 如果我英语好，我会帮你做作业。
  • If I knew her number, I would call her.
  • If I had money, I could buy the car.
  • If I were rich, I might go there.
  • If I knew her number, I wouldcall her.
  • If I had money, I couldbuy the car.
  • If I were rich, I couldgo there.

虚拟语气过去完成时

  • 虚拟语气过去完成时表示对过去事情的假设，也就是对过去没能实现的事情表示后悔
    • 如果我英语及格了，就能通过那次考试了。
    • 如果我认真点学习，那次考试可能就及格了。
  • 虚拟语气过去完成时的表达方式与虚拟语气过去时的表达方式一样，只是决定时态的动词不同
    • 从句一般由if引导，谓语应该用过去完成式had + p.p.；
    • 主句中需有助动词，根据条件状语从句的时态，助动词要用过去完成时态：would, could, might, should + have + p.p.。
  • 翻译句子时要翻译成过去时
    • If I had studied hard, I could have passed the test.
    • If I had known her number, I would have called her.
    • If she had helped me, I would have finished it.
    • If I had got up early, I could have caught the bus.
  • 如果我英语及格了，就能通过那次考试了。
  • 如果我认真点学习，那次考试可能就及格了。
  • 从句一般由if引导，谓语应该用过去完成式had + p.p.；
  • 主句中需有助动词，根据条件状语从句的时态，助动词要用过去完成时态：would, could, might, should + have + p.p.。
  • If I had studied hard, I could have passed the test.
  • If I had known her number, I would have called her.
  • If she had helped me, I would have finished it.
  • If I had got up early, I could have caught the bus.

错综虚拟语气

  • 虚拟语气有时也会不遵守同类原则，即从句和主句所指时间不一致的情况
    • 如果我们早点起床 过去时 ，我们就能看到美丽的日出了。 过去时。
    • 如果我们早点起床 过去时 ，我们现在就在火车上了。 现在时。
  • 当没能实现的事情影响到现在，就要用错综虚拟语气
    • 错综虚拟语气的表达要求如下：条件从句中要有if，主句中要有助动词，这是虚拟语气最基本的条件。if从句中动词用had + p.p.，主句要用助动词的过去式(would, could, might, should)。主句的句末有表示现在的副词now, today, like this等。
  • 如果我们早点起床 过去时 ，我们就能看到美丽的日出了。 过去时。
  • 如果我们早点起床 过去时 ，我们现在就在火车上了。 现在时。
  • 错综虚拟语气的表达要求如下：条件从句中要有if，主句中要有助动词，这是虚拟语气最基本的条件。if从句中动词用had + p.p.，主句要用助动词的过去式(would, could, might, should)。主句的句末有表示现在的副词now, today, like this等。

虚拟语气将来时

  • 虚拟语气将来时表示对将来情况的一种假设
    • 如果他去的几率是一半或更大，这样表达就可以了。
    • 但如果去的几率非常小或几乎没有去的可能时，该如何表达呢？这时就要用虚拟语气将来时。
  • 虚拟语气将来时的表达方式：从句中要有if + should (were to)，主句中要有助动词will(would), can(could), may(might)
    • If it should rain tomorrow, the party will not be held tomorrow.
    • If I were to be born again, I would marry you.
  • if从句中用should时，主句的助动词可以用现在式或过去式。if从句中用were to时，主句的助动词只能用过去，用现在时will, can, may,shall是错误的
    • If it shouldrain tomorrow, I willstay at home.
    • If he shoulddie, I wouldtake care of his children.
    • If I were tobe young again, I wouldmarry you.
    • If the sun were to rise in the west, I wouldmarry you.
  • 如果他去的几率是一半或更大，这样表达就可以了。
  • 但如果去的几率非常小或几乎没有去的可能时，该如何表达呢？这时就要用虚拟语气将来时。
  • If it should rain tomorrow, the party will not be held tomorrow.
  • If I were to be born again, I would marry you.
  • If it shouldrain tomorrow, I willstay at home.
  • If he shoulddie, I wouldtake care of his children.
  • If I were tobe young again, I wouldmarry you.
  • If the sun were to rise in the west, I wouldmarry you.

省略if的情况

  • 为了使句子更简练，虚拟语气通常会省略if。但是如果省略了if，听者或读者该如何判断是虚拟语气呢？为避免引起歧义，省略if时，从句中的主语和谓语要倒装
    • If hehad known the answer, he would have told me.
    • IfI were rich, I could go there.
  • If hehad known the answer, he would have told me.
  • IfI were rich, I could go there.

提高语法水平的处方

  • 将括号中的动词变成恰当的形态
    • If I (know) it well, I could tell you about it.
    • If it (be) fine tomorrow, I will go out.
    • If I (be) smart, I could solve the problem.
    • If I (be) reborn, I would marry you.
    • If she (come), we’ll go out.
    • If it were not so expensive, I (buy) the book.
    • If I (study) harder, I could have passed the test.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • If she had worked hard, she would succeeded.
    • If I don’t do it, my mother would be angry.
    • If he listened to me, he wouldn’t have made a mistake.
    • If you had chose me at that time, you wouldn’t have had any problem.
    • If she has not wasted the money, she could have bought it.
  • 选择恰当的答案
    • If I had a million dollars, I (will / would) buy the car.
    • If you (brought / had brought) the DVD, we could have watched it together.
    • If I (weren’t / hadn’t been) late, my teacher wouldn’t have gotten angry with me.
    • If I had stayed up late last night, I would (be / had been) sleepy now.
    • If I (have / had) a camera, I could take photos.
    • If she (was / were) a student, she could get a student’s discount.
    • If it snowed, I (would make / made) a snowman.
    • If my car weren’t broken, I (can drive / could drive) you home.
    • If he (has / had) a son, he (would play / would played) with him.
  • 选择恰当的答案填空
    • If it __________ rain tomorrow, I would put off my departure.
    • If I __________ again, I would be a doctor like Albert Schweitzer.
    • If my mother had not waked me up in the morning, I __________ longer.
    • The world would be a very uncomfortable place, if there __________ no sun.
    • If you __________ followed his orders, this disaster would not have happened.
    • If another war __________ break out, our civilization would be completely destroyed.
  • 用虚拟语气翻译下列句子
    • 如果我是你，我不会去那里。
    • 如果你做完了作业，我就带你去动物园。
    • 如果我知道答案，我会告诉你。
    • 如果天气再暖和一点，我会去外面。
  • If I (know) it well, I could tell you about it.
  • If it (be) fine tomorrow, I will go out.
  • If I (be) smart, I could solve the problem.
  • If I (be) reborn, I would marry you.
  • If she (come), we’ll go out.
  • If it were not so expensive, I (buy) the book.
  • If I (study) harder, I could have passed the test.
  • If she had worked hard, she would succeeded.
  • If I don’t do it, my mother would be angry.
  • If he listened to me, he wouldn’t have made a mistake.
  • If you had chose me at that time, you wouldn’t have had any problem.
  • If she has not wasted the money, she could have bought it.
  • If I had a million dollars, I (will / would) buy the car.
  • If you (brought / had brought) the DVD, we could have watched it together.
  • If I (weren’t / hadn’t been) late, my teacher wouldn’t have gotten angry with me.
  • If I had stayed up late last night, I would (be / had been) sleepy now.
  • If I (have / had) a camera, I could take photos.
  • If she (was / were) a student, she could get a student’s discount.
  • If it snowed, I (would make / made) a snowman.
  • If my car weren’t broken, I (can drive / could drive) you home.
  • If he (has / had) a son, he (would play / would played) with him.

  • 如果我是你，我不会去那里。

43虚拟语气（其他形式） ›

共 2 个小节 · 7 条要点 · 27 句原句摘录

虚拟语气(其他形式)

  • I wish虚拟语气用于表达对过去或现在的后悔或愿望
    • I wish用于虚拟语气过去时表示对现在情况的遗憾或愿望，意思是“如果……就好了”。
    • I wish虚拟语气过去完成时表示对过去没能实现的事情的后悔或期望，意思是“如果……就好了”。
  • as if虚拟语气用于讽刺与现在事实相反的内容或过去事实相反的情况
    • as if用于虚拟语气过去时表示讽刺与现在事实相反的内容，意思是“就像……一样”，翻译成现在时。
    • as if用于虚拟语气过去完成时表示与过去事实相反的情况，意思是“好像……一样”，翻译成过去时。
  • but for(=without)虚拟语气假设的是“没有什么”，翻译成“如果没有过……(过去时)”“如果没有……(现在时)”
    • but for虚拟语气过去时的意思是“如果没有……”，也要翻译成现在时。
    • but for用于虚拟语气过去完成时的意思是“如果没有过……”，翻译成过去时。
  • I wish用于虚拟语气过去时表示对现在情况的遗憾或愿望，意思是“如果……就好了”。
  • I wish虚拟语气过去完成时表示对过去没能实现的事情的后悔或期望，意思是“如果……就好了”。
  • as if用于虚拟语气过去时表示讽刺与现在事实相反的内容，意思是“就像……一样”，翻译成现在时。
  • as if用于虚拟语气过去完成时表示与过去事实相反的情况，意思是“好像……一样”，翻译成过去时。
  • but for虚拟语气过去时的意思是“如果没有……”，也要翻译成现在时。
  • but for用于虚拟语气过去完成时的意思是“如果没有过……”，翻译成过去时。

提高语法水平的处方

  • 把下列句子改成直接陈述句
    • As I don’t know her address, I cannot write to her.
    • As I didn’t know her address, I didn’t write to her.
    • I am sorry I can’t speak Japanese.
    • I am sorry she didn’t come to the meeting yesterday.
    • In fact, she is not rich.
    • In fact, he was not a doctor.
  • 将下列句子改写成if虚拟语气
    • If it were not for water, no one could live on the earth.
    • If it had not been for your help, I could not have finished the work.
  • 将下列句子改写成省略if的虚拟语气
    • Were I rich, I would buy the house.
    • Should I die, what would become of my wife?
    • Had I studied harder, I would have passed the test.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • know → knew / had know，可以用过去时，也可以用过去完成时。
    • drank → had drunk，要符合同类原则。
    • is → were 4. had → had had，有确切的过去时间点yesterday，所以要用过去完成时。
    • have → had / had had 6. was → were / had been 7. miss → had missed。
    • didn’t eat → hadn’t eaten，后悔过去的事情，所以应该用虚拟语气过去完成时。
    • were → had been 10. could not do → could not have done。
    • were not → had not been；couldn’t have done → couldn’t do。
    • are → were，有now所以只能用虚拟语气过去时。
    • can’t complete → couldn’t have completed，有last week所以只能用虚拟语气过去完成时。
    • are → were，现在的事实，所以只能用虚拟语气过去时。
  • As I don’t know her address, I cannot write to her.
  • As I didn’t know her address, I didn’t write to her.
  • I am sorry I can’t speak Japanese.
  • I am sorry she didn’t come to the meeting yesterday.
  • In fact, she is not rich.
  • In fact, he was not a doctor.
  • If it were not for water, no one could live on the earth.
  • If it had not been for your help, I could not have finished the work.
  • Were I rich, I would buy the house.
  • Should I die, what would become of my wife?
  • Had I studied harder, I would have passed the test.
  • know → knew / had know，可以用过去时，也可以用过去完成时。
  • drank → had drunk，要符合同类原则。
  • is → were 4. had → had had，有确切的过去时间点yesterday，所以要用过去完成时。
  • have → had / had had 6. was → were / had been 7. miss → had missed。
  • didn’t eat → hadn’t eaten，后悔过去的事情，所以应该用虚拟语气过去完成时。
  • were → had been 10. could not do → could not have done。
  • were not → had not been；couldn’t have done → couldn’t do。
  • are → were，有now所以只能用虚拟语气过去时。
  • can’t complete → couldn’t have completed，有last week所以只能用虚拟语气过去完成时。
  • are → were，现在的事实，所以只能用虚拟语气过去时。
44Part 10 时态和引语 ›

共 2 个小节 · 4 条要点 · 11 句原句摘录

诊断你的语法水平

  • 找出错误并改正
    • The child is ill for the past ten days. (is → has been)。
    • I have just now arrived in Canada. (just now → just)。
    • The airplane will reach New York by this day week. (will reach → will have reached)。
    • I learned that twice three was six. (was → is)。
    • The beautiful gardens are belonging to my grandfather. (are belonging → belong)。
    • I don’t know if it rains, but if it will do, I shall stay at home. (will do → does)。
    • By the time you will receive this letter, everything will be settled. (will receive → receive)。
    • The road was muddy, as it rained the previous day. (rained → had rained)。
  • 选择恰当的答案
    • He said that honesty (is / was) the best policy. (is)。
    • They (have come / came) three years ago. (came)。
    • We (have rented / rented) our apartment since 1990. (have rented)。
  • The child is ill for the past ten days. (is → has been)。
  • I have just now arrived in Canada. (just now → just)。
  • The airplane will reach New York by this day week. (will reach → will have reached)。
  • I learned that twice three was six. (was → is)。
  • The beautiful gardens are belonging to my grandfather. (are belonging → belong)。
  • I don’t know if it rains, but if it will do, I shall stay at home. (will do → does)。
  • By the time you will receive this letter, everything will be settled. (will receive → receive)。
  • The road was muddy, as it rained the previous day. (rained → had rained)。
  • He said that honesty (is / was) the best policy. (is)。
  • They (have come / came) three years ago. (came)。
  • We (have rented / rented) our apartment since 1990. (have rented)。

掌握英语时态的概念

  • 时态是根据动作发生的时间，大致可以分为过去、现在和将来三种，与一般式、进行式、完成式和完成进行式组成时态。
  • 动词决定时态，所以根据动词的变化可判断时态，反之，也可以根据时态来选择动词。
4512种基本时态 ›

共 12 个小节 · 21 条要点 · 35 句原句摘录

一般过去时

  • 表示过去发生的事情
    • 谓语为be动词或实义动词的过去式时，句子就是一般过去时。
  • 一般过去时和现在完成时的区别
    • 一般过去时是指某种情况或行为在过去的特定时间点发生，并在过去结束。
    • 现在完成时是指过去某个时间点发生的事情对现在依然有影响，并不一定结束。
  • 谓语为be动词或实义动词的过去式时，句子就是一般过去时。
  • 一般过去时是指某种情况或行为在过去的特定时间点发生，并在过去结束。
  • 现在完成时是指过去某个时间点发生的事情对现在依然有影响，并不一定结束。

一般现在时

  • 表示现在发生的事情
    • 谓语为be动词或实义动词的现在形式，就是现在时。
  • 表示现在时间点的副词now能让句子意思更加明确
    • 第三人称单数且时态为一般现在时，实义动词后要加-s。
    • 不变的真理用一般现在时表达。
  • 谓语为be动词或实义动词的现在形式，就是现在时。
  • 第三人称单数且时态为一般现在时，实义动词后要加-s。
  • 不变的真理用一般现在时表达。

一般将来时

  • 表示将来发生的事情
    • 将来时需要用助动词will或be going to。
  • 表示将来时间的副词，如tomorrow, this week, next week, this year, next year等，可以让将来时态的句子意思更明确。
  • 将来时需要用助动词will或be going to。

过去进行时

  • 用于表示在过去特定的某个时间点正在进行的某件事
    • 翻译成“正在做……”，表达形式是 was / were + V-ing。
  • 如果只有过去进行时，虽然不是错误的句子，但是加上表示特定时间的修饰语，传达的意思才够明确
    • I wascleaning my car when it begun to rain. 开始下雨时，我正在洗车。
    • I waswatching television when he came in. 他进来时，我正在看电视。
  • 翻译成“正在做……”，表达形式是 was / were + V-ing。
  • I wascleaning my car when it begun to rain. 开始下雨时，我正在洗车。
  • I waswatching television when he came in. 他进来时，我正在看电视。

现在进行时

  • 用于表示现在正在做某事或某行为
    • 意思是“正在做……”，表达形式是 be +V-ing。
  • 有些动词不能用于进行式
    • 例如一些表示拥有或状态的动词。
  • 现在进行时与表示将来的副词tomorrow, this, week, next week, this year,next year等连用，可表示将来的意思
    • The train is leaving tomorrow.火车明天离开。
    • Tiger Woods is leaving New York tonight. 泰格·伍兹今晚离开纽约。
  • 意思是“正在做……”，表达形式是 be +V-ing。
  • 例如一些表示拥有或状态的动词。
  • The train is leaving tomorrow.火车明天离开。
  • Tiger Woods is leaving New York tonight. 泰格·伍兹今晚离开纽约。

将来进行时

  • 用于表示在将来的特定时间点正在做或连续做某事
    • 意思是“会在做……”，表达形式是 will be + V-ing。
  • 为了使意思更加明确，需要与表示将来特定时间点的副词连用。
  • 意思是“会在做……”，表达形式是 will be + V-ing。

过去完成时

  • 过去完成时表示在过去某一时间或动作以前已经发生或完成的动作，对过去某一点造成影响，有持续、经验、完成、结果四种用法
    • 持续：He hadlived in Chicago before he moved to New York last year.
    • 经验：Had you ever seen the man before then?
    • 完成：I had just finished my homework when he came in.
    • 结果：My father hadgone to America when I was born.
  • 和其他时态一样，单独使用过去完成时也不能明确表达意思，所以过去完成时也需要表示时间的副词
    • He hadlived in Chicago. (×)。
    • Had you ever seen the man before? (×)。
    • I had just finished my homework. (×)。
    • My father hadgone to America. (×)。
  • 持续：He hadlived in Chicago before he moved to New York last year.
  • 经验：Had you ever seen the man before then?
  • 完成：I had just finished my homework when he came in.
  • 结果：My father hadgone to America when I was born.
  • He hadlived in Chicago. (×)。
  • Had you ever seen the man before? (×)。
  • I had just finished my homework. (×)。
  • My father hadgone to America. (×)。

现在完成时

  • 用于表示过去某个时间发生的事情一直持续到现在，与过去完成时一样，也有持续、经验、完成、结果四种用法
    • 持续：She haslived here since 2000.
    • 经验：Have you ever used this program before?
    • 完成：I have just finished my homework.
    • 结果：She hasgone to Japan. (√)。
    • 使用现在完成时的持续用法时，如“我从2001年开始一直住在这里。”比“我一直住在这里。”表达的意思更明确。所以，现在完成时的持续用法一般与表示特定时间起点的副词since, for等连用。
  • just和just now的区别
    • just多用于现在完成时中强调刚刚结束。
    • just now用于一般过去时中，强调过去。
  • 持续：She haslived here since 2000.
  • 经验：Have you ever used this program before?
  • 完成：I have just finished my homework.
  • 结果：She hasgone to Japan. (√)。
  • 使用现在完成时的持续用法时，如“我从2001年开始一直住在这里。”比“我一直住在这里。”表达的意思更明确。所以，现在完成时的持续用法一般与表示特定时间起点的副词since, for等连用。
  • just多用于现在完成时中强调刚刚结束。
  • just now用于一般过去时中，强调过去。

将来完成时

  • 用于表示在将来的特定时间点前开始的事情在将来的另一特定时间点结束，意思是“将完成……”
    • 为了让意思更明确，将来完成时也要和表示特定将来时间点的副词一起用。
  • 为了让意思更明确，将来完成时也要和表示特定将来时间点的副词一起用。

过去完成进行时

  • 用于表示动作在过去某一时间之前开始，一直延续到这一过去时间仍然在进行或者刚刚结束，意思是“那时正在做……”
    • 为了让意思更明确，过去完成进行时也要和表示特定过去时间点的副词一起用。
  • 为了让意思更明确，过去完成进行时也要和表示特定过去时间点的副词一起用。

现在完成进行时

  • 用于表示从过去的特定时间点开始一直持续到现在
    • 表达形式是 have been +V-ing，有持续、经验、完成、结果四种用法。
  • 表达形式是 have been +V-ing，有持续、经验、完成、结果四种用法。

将来完成进行时

  • 用于表示从某个将来的特定时间点前开始，一直持续到特定时间点结束
    • 意思是“会在做……”，表达形式是 will have been + V-ing。
    • 为了让意思更明确，将来完成进行时也要和表示将来特定时间点的副词一起用。
  • 意思是“会在做……”，表达形式是 will have been + V-ing。
  • 为了让意思更明确，将来完成进行时也要和表示将来特定时间点的副词一起用。
46引语 ›

共 3 个小节 · 10 条要点 · 42 句原句摘录

引语的概念

  • 直接引语是双引号直接原封不动地引用从别人那里听来的话。
  • 间接引语是站在转达者的立场上，适当改变后转达从别人那里听到的内容。

直接引语变成间接引语的方法

  • 陈述句中引语的转换规则
    • 去掉逗号和引号，用that代替。that可以省略。
    • 使用正确的表示转达的动词。(say to → tell, said to → told等)。
    • 站在转达者的立场改变直接引语的主语、宾语等。(I → he等)。
    • 如果主句的谓语是现在时，不用改变直接引语的时态；如果主句的谓语是过去时，就要把直接引语中的现在时变成过去时，过去时变成过去完成时，现在完成时变成过去完成时。也就是说时态要一致。
    • 还要站在转达者的立场上改变形容词或副词。(表示转达的动词是过去时的时候) Now → then, this → that, ago → before。
  • 疑问句中引语的转换规则
    • 一般疑问句，把表示转达的动词say(to)变成ask。
    • 把要转述的句子的语序变为“if / whether +主语+谓语”。
    • 特殊疑问句，把表示转达的动词say(to)变成ask。
    • 把要转述的句子的语序变为“特殊疑问词+主语+谓语”。
  • 祈使句中引语的转换规则
    • 把表示转达的动词say(to)变成tell(告诉)，order(命令)，ask(请求)，advise(建议)或beg(恳请)等。
    • 把要转达的句子(祈使句)变成动词不定式。
  • 去掉逗号和引号，用that代替。that可以省略。
  • 使用正确的表示转达的动词。(say to → tell, said to → told等)。
  • 站在转达者的立场改变直接引语的主语、宾语等。(I → he等)。
  • 如果主句的谓语是现在时，不用改变直接引语的时态；如果主句的谓语是过去时，就要把直接引语中的现在时变成过去时，过去时变成过去完成时，现在完成时变成过去完成时。也就是说时态要一致。
  • 还要站在转达者的立场上改变形容词或副词。(表示转达的动词是过去时的时候) Now → then, this → that, ago → before。
  • 一般疑问句，把表示转达的动词say(to)变成ask。
  • 把要转述的句子的语序变为“if / whether +主语+谓语”。
  • 特殊疑问句，把表示转达的动词say(to)变成ask。
  • 把要转述的句子的语序变为“特殊疑问词+主语+谓语”。
  • 把表示转达的动词say(to)变成tell(告诉)，order(命令)，ask(请求)，advise(建议)或beg(恳请)等。
  • 把要转达的句子(祈使句)变成动词不定式。

提高语法水平的处方

  • 找出错误并改正
    • have you gone → did you go，不能用I have gone..., you have gone...。
    • do not → have not，有just, now, already, yet就是现在完成时的完成用法。
    • it have → it has，since要与现在完成时一起用。
    • gone → been，表示“去过”用been to...。
    • till → by，by表示完成，till表示持续。
    • will come → comes，时间状语从句中要用现在时代替将来时。
    • is standing → stands，表示拥有或状态的动词不能用于进行时。
    • has gone → went，有表示特定过去的five days ago，所以只能用过去时。
    • will climb → will have climbed， 将来完成时表示经验的用法。
    • will have done → have done，时间状语从句中要用现在时代替将来时。
    • has not gone → had not gone。
  • 选择恰当的答案
    • gains，俗语通常用现在时。
    • do not bite。
    • has lived，这是现在完成时的持续用法。
    • bought，有表示特定过去时间的last year，所以只能用过去时。
  • 回答下列问题
    • till后是时间状语从句，要用现在时代替将来时，所以答案是①。
    • till后是时间状语从句，要用现在时代替将来时，所以答案是④，应该把will stop改成stops。
    • 表示动作的动词可以用进行时，但表示拥有或状态的动词不能用进行时。③中have表示“拥有”的意思，不能用进行时，所以答案是③。
  • 选择恰当的答案填空
    • 这个句子可以翻译为“你老了会需要一个可以依靠的人。”时间状语从句中要用现在时代替将来时，所以答案是③。
    • 这个句子可以翻译为“到明年12月，迈克就在墨西哥生活13年了。”这是将来完成时，所以答案是⑤。
    • 这个句子可以翻译为“艾伦3个小时一直在写同一封信，而且他还没写完。”现在完成进行时，所以答案是①。
    • 这个句子可以翻译为“到明年，金教授就教10年英语了。”这个句子是将来完成进行时，所以答案是①。
  • 把下列句子变成间接引语
    • She said that she could not play that day as she had an appointment.
    • He said that he would be back as soon as he could.
    • He said that he had been very busy a few days before but he was quite free then.
    • Our teacher told us that the earth is round.
    • He said that he gets up at six every morning.
    • He said that his brother had already started.
    • He says that he is happy.
    • I said that I was sleepy.
    • He told me that he had written me a letter.

47Part 11 It’s 引导的特殊句型 ›

共 1 个小节 · 2 条要点 · 6 句原句摘录

It’s time...句型的概念和用法

  • It’s time...意思是“该做……了”
    • It’s time for you to do your homework.
    • It is about time he introduced himself to us.
  • It’s time...句型可以用虚拟语气，也可以不用
    • It’s time that you did your homework.
    • It’s time that you should do your homework.
    • It is about time him to introduce himself to us.
    • It is about time he should introduce himself to us.
  • It’s time for you to do your homework.
  • It is about time he introduced himself to us.
  • It’s time that you did your homework.
  • It’s time that you should do your homework.
  • It is about time him to introduce himself to us.
  • It is about time he should introduce himself to us.
48It’s time句型 ›

共 7 个小节 · 19 条要点 · 15 句原句摘录

It is time +动词不定式

  • It is time to go表示“该走了。”。
  • It is time to go to bed表示“该睡觉了。”。
  • 可以在动词不定式前加上“for +宾格”
    • It is time foryou to go to bed. 你该睡觉了。
    • It is time forus to go to bed. 我们该睡觉了。
  • It is time foryou to go to bed. 你该睡觉了。
  • It is time forus to go to bed. 我们该睡觉了。

It is time +从句

  • It is time后也可以接从句，谓语用动词的过去式，但是表达的意思是现在时
    • It is time that you went to bed.
    • It is time后是由that引导、含主语和谓语的主语从句。that是关系副词。
  • “It’s time +从句”多表达责备或不满
    • 用于还没有发生的事情或情况，尽管和虚拟语气很相似，但并不是虚拟语气。
  • It is time that you went to bed.
  • It is time后是由that引导、含主语和谓语的主语从句。that是关系副词。
  • 用于还没有发生的事情或情况，尽管和虚拟语气很相似，但并不是虚拟语气。

虚拟语气和It’s time...句型的共同点和区别

  • 共同点：表达的都是没有发生的事情，都用动词的过去式，但翻译成现在时
    • 虚拟语气遵循同类原则，但是It’s time...句型不遵循此原则。
    • 虚拟语气的从句可以用过去完成时、过去时、现在时，但It’s time...句型后一般只用动词的过去。
  • 区别：虚拟语气中主句可以是现在时，从句是没有特定过去基准点的过去完成时；It’s time...句型后要接过去时，be动词要根据人称变化
    • 虚拟语气过去时中，be动词都用were。
    • It’s time...句型中，be动词要随人称变化。
  • 虚拟语气遵循同类原则，但是It’s time...句型不遵循此原则。
  • 虚拟语气的从句可以用过去完成时、过去时、现在时，但It’s time...句型后一般只用动词的过去。
  • 虚拟语气过去时中，be动词都用were。
  • It’s time...句型中，be动词要随人称变化。

It’s time...句型的表达方式

  • It’s time for you to go to bed.(动词不定式的形容词性用法)。
  • It’s time that you went to bed.(It’s time...句型)。
  • It’s time that you shouldgo to bed.(使用助动词should)。
  • It’s time that you go to bed.(省略助动词should)。
  • It’s time you went to bed.(关系副词that可以省略，所以省略了②中的that)。
  • It’s time you shouldgo to bed.(省略了③中的that)。
  • It’s time you go to bed.(省略了④中的that)。

It’s time...句型的例句

  • It’s high time (that) you took care of your brother.

It is time that句型的用法

  • It is time that句型用于表示对现在还没发生的事情的责备、不满和焦虑
    • 用于表示还没发生的事情，这一点与虚拟语气相同，但并不是表示希望或假设的虚拟语气，所以不能与虚拟语气混淆。
  • It is time that后要用过去时，be动词要随人称变化
    • 在虚拟语气过去时中，be动词都要用were，但是在It’s time that句型中，be动词要随人称变化。
  • “It is time +动词过去式”应该翻译成现在时，意思是“该做……了”
    • 这个句子的意思是“他该采取强烈的措施抵制他们了。”。
  • 用于表示还没发生的事情，这一点与虚拟语气相同，但并不是表示希望或假设的虚拟语气，所以不能与虚拟语气混淆。
  • 在虚拟语气过去时中，be动词都要用were，但是在It’s time that句型中，be动词要随人称变化。
  • 这个句子的意思是“他该采取强烈的措施抵制他们了。”。

It is time that句型的例句解析

  • He is already on the wrong side of forties. It’s about time he found himself a wife and settled down
    • 他已经40多岁了。他该结婚安定下来了。
    • 是It is time that句型，所以从句后要用过去时。
    • 由并列连词and连接，所以前后都应该用过去时(found, settled)。所以答案是③。
  • 他已经40多岁了。他该结婚安定下来了。
  • 是It is time that句型，所以从句后要用过去时。
  • 由并列连词and连接，所以前后都应该用过去时(found, settled)。所以答案是③。
49忠主要命提 + should句型 ›

共 2 个小节 · 4 条要点 · 15 句原句摘录

“忠主要命提 + should句型”的用法

  • “忠主要命提”的句子中，that从句中必须用should
    • “你该睡觉了。”表达的是你应该去睡觉了，但是还没有去睡觉。而would没有“必须做……”的意思，所以不能代替should。
    • shall也不能代替should，是因为表达的是过去应该做但是还没做，所以必须用过去式。
  • 在表示忠告、主张、要求、命令、提议时，that从句中也要用should
    • 比如，一位医生对肥胖症患者说：“如果你不赶快减肥，会出大事的。”。
    • 史莱克虽然深爱着菲奥娜公主，但是因为自己长得不够英俊，没有勇气再去见菲奥娜公主。所以朋友们对史莱克说：“史莱克，鼓起勇气，你该去见菲奥娜公主！”。
    • 史莱克只顾着玩游戏，在期末考试中成绩一落千丈，老师看到考试成绩后要求史莱克：“你认真点学习，别只顾着玩游戏！”。
    • 史莱克胃痛难忍，去医院接受治疗，医生对史莱克说“想治好胃病得吃这个药，一天三次！”。
    • 菲奥娜公主减肥过度忽然晕倒，被送到了医院。虽然医生劝她不要再减肥了，要好好吃饭，但是菲奥娜公主固执地坚持继续减肥。所以医生建议她只吃晚饭。
  • “你该睡觉了。”表达的是你应该去睡觉了，但是还没有去睡觉。而would没有“必须做……”的意思，所以不能代替should。
  • shall也不能代替should，是因为表达的是过去应该做但是还没做，所以必须用过去式。
  • 比如，一位医生对肥胖症患者说：“如果你不赶快减肥，会出大事的。”。
  • 史莱克虽然深爱着菲奥娜公主，但是因为自己长得不够英俊，没有勇气再去见菲奥娜公主。所以朋友们对史莱克说：“史莱克，鼓起勇气，你该去见菲奥娜公主！”。
  • 史莱克只顾着玩游戏，在期末考试中成绩一落千丈，老师看到考试成绩后要求史莱克：“你认真点学习，别只顾着玩游戏！”。
  • 史莱克胃痛难忍，去医院接受治疗，医生对史莱克说“想治好胃病得吃这个药，一天三次！”。
  • 菲奥娜公主减肥过度忽然晕倒，被送到了医院。虽然医生劝她不要再减肥了，要好好吃饭，但是菲奥娜公主固执地坚持继续减肥。所以医生建议她只吃晚饭。

“忠主要命提 + that引导的名词性从句”的用法

  • 如果主句含有表示忠主要命提的动词，that从句中的内容不是过去发生的事情，而是表示“必须做”的意思时，一定要用“should+动词原形”
    • I insist that he (should) go with us. 我认为他应该和我们一起走。
    • We demanded that he (should) pay the money. 我们要求他付钱。
    • The doctor insists that she (should) give up smoking. 医生认为她应该戒烟。
    • He suggested that the party (should) be put off. 他建议派对应该被延期。
  • 表示“忠主要命提”的动词都含有义务的意思，为避免重复，可以省略同样含有义务意思的should
    • I insist that he goes with us. (×)。
    • We demanded that he pays the money. (×)。
    • The doctor insists that she gives up smoking. (×)。
    • He suggested that the party is put off. (×)。
  • I insist that he (should) go with us. 我认为他应该和我们一起走。
  • We demanded that he (should) pay the money. 我们要求他付钱。
  • The doctor insists that she (should) give up smoking. 医生认为她应该戒烟。
  • He suggested that the party (should) be put off. 他建议派对应该被延期。
  • I insist that he goes with us. (×)。
  • We demanded that he pays the money. (×)。
  • The doctor insists that she gives up smoking. (×)。
  • He suggested that the party is put off. (×)。
50It’s +判断 / 感情形容词+ that + should ›

共 3 个小节 · 3 条要点 · 8 句原句摘录

四种句型：It is time that + 主语 + should + 动词；主语+忠主要命提 that 主语 + should + 动词；It is 判断形容词that 主语 + should + 动词；It is 感情形容词that 主语 + should + 动词

  • 在这些句型中，that从句中用should必须满足两个条件：还未发生的事情且含有“必须做……”的含义。

文章通过例句和练习题，帮助读者理解和掌握这些句型的使用

  • 例句和练习题展示了如何使用这些句型
    • 例句1：It is time that we should start our work.
    • 例句2：I insist that he should go with us.
    • 例句3：It is necessary that we should have a meeting.
    • 例句4：It is surprising that he should have won the prize.
    • 练习题：用适当的句型转换句子。
  • 例句1：It is time that we should start our work.
  • 例句2：I insist that he should go with us.
  • 例句3：It is necessary that we should have a meeting.
  • 例句4：It is surprising that he should have won the prize.
  • 练习题：用适当的句型转换句子。

文章给出了一些提高语法水平的建议

  • 包括填空、选择语法正确的句子、找出语法错误的句子并改正等
    • 填空：用适当的句型填空。
    • 选择：选择语法正确的句子。
    • 找出并改正：找出语法错误的句子并改正。
  • 填空：用适当的句型填空。
  • 选择：选择语法正确的句子。
  • 找出并改正：找出语法错误的句子并改正。
51Part 12 被动语态 ›

共 4 个小节 · 21 条要点 · 3 句原句摘录

将下列句子改写成被动语态

  • All Koreans love her. - She is loved by all Koreans.
  • Someone stole my car. - My car was stolen by someone.
  • He will write the letter tomorrow. - The letter will be written tomorrow by him.
  • They are painting the door. - The door is being painted by them.
  • She was singing the song. - The song was being sung by her.
  • He has repaired my watch. - My watch has been repaired by him.
  • Mom gave me an apple. - I was given an apple by Mom. / An apple was given (to) me by Mom.
  • Dad bought me the bike. - The bike was bought for me by Dad.
  • I saw him enter the house. - He was seen to enter the house by me.
  • I made my car washed. - My car was made to be washed by me.

填写正确的介词

  • The room is filled with trash.
  • She is well known to neighbors.
  • I am interested in classical music.
  • He was surprised at the result.
  • This chair is made of metal.

找出错误并改正

  • I was read a book by my mom. - A book was read (to) me by my mom.
  • He was made study harder by his dad. - He was made to study harder by his dad.
  • The baby is taken care by her. - The baby is taken care of by her.
  • Wine is made of grapes. - Wine is made from grapes.
  • She is resembled by her brother. - Her brother resembles her.

被动语态的概念

  • 主动语态表示主语是动作的执行者，被动语态表示主语是动作的承受者
    • 我要认真学习！- 我认真学习了！(在爸爸的施压下)。
    • 他认真工作！- 他认真工作了！(在老板的施压下)。
    • 我的宠物狗拼力地跑！- 我的宠物狗拼力地跑了！(在我的驱使下)。
  • 我要认真学习！- 我认真学习了！(在爸爸的施压下)。
  • 他认真工作！- 他认真工作了！(在老板的施压下)。
  • 我的宠物狗拼力地跑！- 我的宠物狗拼力地跑了！(在我的驱使下)。
52被动语态的结构 ›

共 2 个小节 · 4 条要点 · 5 句原句摘录

被动语态的结构

  • 英语中用be + p.p.表示被动语态
    • I love her. 主动语态 → She is loved by me. 被动语态。
  • 在被动语态的句子中可以同时存在be动词和实义动词，只不过实义动词需要变形
    • I amliving in Seoul. 现在进行时 → I am being lived in Seoul. 被动语态。
    • I am drinking milk. 现在进行时 → I am being drunk milk. 被动语态。
  • I love her. 主动语态 → She is loved by me. 被动语态。
  • I amliving in Seoul. 现在进行时 → I am being lived in Seoul. 被动语态。
  • I am drinking milk. 现在进行时 → I am being drunk milk. 被动语态。

被动语态的表达方式

  • 动作不是由主语自己完成的，而是由其他人完成的，就用被动语态
    • 主动语态的句子变成被动语态时，前提条件就是原来的句子中要有宾语。
  • 有宾语的句子是第三类句型、第四类句型和第五类句型，它们可以变成被动语态
    • 第一类句型 S + V 和第二类句型 S + V + P 没有宾语，所以它们无法变成被动语态。
  • 主动语态的句子变成被动语态时，前提条件就是原来的句子中要有宾语。
  • 第一类句型 S + V 和第二类句型 S + V + P 没有宾语，所以它们无法变成被动语态。
53第三类句型的被动语态 ›

共 9 个小节 · 10 条要点 · 12 句原句摘录

第三类句型的被动语态结构

  • 第三类句型的结构是“主语+谓语+宾语”，变成被动语态就是“什么(宾语)被我(主语)做”
    • 主语语态中的宾语变成了被动语态的主语。
    • 主动语态中的谓语变成被动语态的be + p.p.。
    • 主动语态中的主语在被动语态中变成by +宾语。
  • 主语语态中的宾语变成了被动语态的主语。
  • 主动语态中的谓语变成被动语态的be + p.p.。
  • 主动语态中的主语在被动语态中变成by +宾语。

现在时的被动语态

  • 把宾语放到句首，然后把谓语变成be + p.p.，最后把主动语态的主语变成“by +宾语”
    • 例如：Sarah teaches English. → English is taught by Sarah.
  • 例如：Sarah teaches English. → English is taught by Sarah.

过去时的被动语态

  • 把宾语放到前面，谓语变成be＋p.p.，将原来的主语变成“by＋宾格”
    • 例如：Columbus discovered the America. → The Americas was discovered by Columbus.
  • 例如：Columbus discovered the America. → The Americas was discovered by Columbus.

将来时的被动语态

  • 先把宾语放到前面，然后把谓语变成be + p.p.，最后把原来的主语变成“by +宾语”
    • 例如：The work will be finished by her.
  • 例如：The work will be finished by her.

进行时的被动语态

  • 把宾语移到前面，把谓语变成be + being＋p.p.，最后把原来的主语变成“by +宾语”
    • 例如：They are building the house. → The house is being built by them.
  • 例如：They are building the house. → The house is being built by them.

完成时的被动语态

  • 把宾语移到前面，把谓语变成have / has been＋p.p.，最后把原来的主语变成“by +宾语”
    • 例如：Jane has cleaned the room. → The room has been cleaned by Jane.
  • 例如：Jane has cleaned the room. → The room has been cleaned by Jane.

否定句的被动语态

  • 把宾语移到前面，把谓语变成be + not + p.p.，最后把原来的主语变成“by +宾语”
    • 例如：She did not write the card. → The card was not written by her.
  • 例如：She did not write the card. → The card was not written by her.

含助动词的被动语态

  • 把宾语移到前面，把谓语变成助动词＋be＋p.p.，最后把原来的主语变成“by +宾语”
    • 例如：She cannot play the piano. → The piano cannot be played by her.
  • 例如：She cannot play the piano. → The piano cannot be played by her.

疑问句的被动语态

  • 一般疑问句变成被动语态时，通常都是像这样变成一般疑问句
    • 例如：Did you find book? → Was the book found by you?
  • 特殊疑问句变成被动语态时，特殊疑问词一般放在句首
    • 例如：When did you build this house? → When was this house built by you?
  • 例如：Did you find book? → Was the book found by you?
  • 例如：When did you build this house? → When was this house built by you?
54第四类句型的被动语态 ›

共 3 个小节 · 5 条要点 · 6 句原句摘录

第四类句型的被动语态

  • 第四类句型有两个宾语，变成被动语态时有两种方法，一种是人作主语，一种是事物作主语
    • 英语由她教我们。The book was given Shrek (by you)。
    • 为了更明确地表达出“给某人”，可以在“某人”前加上to。
  • 英语由她教我们。The book was given Shrek (by you)。
  • 为了更明确地表达出“给某人”，可以在“某人”前加上to。

第四类句型变为被动语态时的注意事项

  • 事物作主语的被动语态，为了明确表示给某人，可以在人(间接宾语)前加上to
    • A cake was made to me by her. (×) 正确的句子应该是A cake was made for me by her.
    • 第四类句型的被动语态中，事物作主语时，谓语是某些特定的动词时，要用for代替to。
  • 人作主语的被动语态，含有某些动词的第四类句型是不能变成人作主语的被动语态的
    • She made me a cake. 她给我做了一个蛋糕。这个句子的意思是“我被她做了一个蛋糕。”。
    • 用这些动词作谓语的第四类句型变成被动语态时，只能变成事物作主语的被动语态。
  • A cake was made to me by her. (×) 正确的句子应该是A cake was made for me by her.
  • 第四类句型的被动语态中，事物作主语时，谓语是某些特定的动词时，要用for代替to。
  • She made me a cake. 她给我做了一个蛋糕。这个句子的意思是“我被她做了一个蛋糕。”。
  • 用这些动词作谓语的第四类句型变成被动语态时，只能变成事物作主语的被动语态。

将下列句子改写成被动语态

  • Mom gave me an apple. 人作主语I was given an apple by Mom. 事物作主语An apple was given (to) me by Mom.
  • Dad bought me the bike. 使用buy作谓语的第四类句型变成被动语态时，不能用人作主语。事物作主语The bike was bought for me by Dad.
55第五类句型的被动语态 ›

共 7 个小节 · 10 条要点 · 27 句原句摘录

第五类句型的被动语态

  • 把宾语移到前面，把动词变成be + p.p.，把宾语补足语放在谓语后，把主语变成“by +宾语”
    • I... → I am called... → I am called a genius... → I am called a genius by them...
  • make在第四类句型中作授予动词时，表示“让……”，所以不能变成人作主语的被动语态。但是make在第三类句型或第五类句型中，可以变成人作主语的被动语态
    • I think him a doctor. → He is thought a doctor by me.
    • We called him a spy. → He was called a spy by us.
    • We elected Bush a President. → Bush was elected a President by us.
    • You make me crazy. → I am made crazy by you.
  • I... → I am called... → I am called a genius... → I am called a genius by them...
  • I think him a doctor. → He is thought a doctor by me.
  • We called him a spy. → He was called a spy by us.
  • We elected Bush a President. → Bush was elected a President by us.
  • You make me crazy. → I am made crazy by you.

含感官动词、使役动词的被动语态

  • 在含感官动词或使役动词的第五类句型的句子中，动词可以作补语
    • We saw him dance. (√) → 用动词作宾语补足语，但saw(see的过去式)是感官动词，所以是正确的句子。
    • I heard you shout. (√) → 用动词作宾语补足语，但heard(hear的过去式)是感官动词，所以是正确的句子。
    • You let her go.(√)→用动词作宾语补足语，但let是使役动词，所以是正确的句子。
    • Shrek makes Princess Fiona wash his car. (√) → 用动词作宾语补足语，但makes是使役动词，所以是正确的句子。
  • 把含感官动词和使役动词的第五类句型变成被动语态时，一定要在作宾语补足语的动词前加上to
    • She made me work hard. → I was made to work hard by her.
    • We saw him break the window. → He was seen to break the window by us.
  • We saw him dance. (√) → 用动词作宾语补足语，但saw(see的过去式)是感官动词，所以是正确的句子。
  • I heard you shout. (√) → 用动词作宾语补足语，但heard(hear的过去式)是感官动词，所以是正确的句子。
  • You let her go.(√)→用动词作宾语补足语，但let是使役动词，所以是正确的句子。
  • Shrek makes Princess Fiona wash his car. (√) → 用动词作宾语补足语，但makes是使役动词，所以是正确的句子。
  • She made me work hard. → I was made to work hard by her.
  • We saw him break the window. → He was seen to break the window by us.

通过解题治疗语法5

  • 将下列句子改写成被动语态
    • I made him repair my watch. → I made my watch repaired by him.
    • I made her paint the door. → I made the door painted by her.
  • I made him repair my watch. → I made my watch repaired by him.
  • I made her paint the door. → I made the door painted by her.

治疗你的语法水平

  • 第五类句型变成被动语态的知识汇总
    • We call this puppy Happy. (名词作补语，小狗的名字叫Happy。) → This puppy is called Happy by us.
    • This movie made us happy. (使役动词，形容词作补语。) → We were made to happy by this movie.
    • Mom made us study hard.(使役动词，动词原形作补语。) → We were made to study hard by Mom.
    • I had the box carried by the boy.(使役动词，过去分词作补语。) → The box was had to be carried by the boy by me. (×) → The box was made to be carried by the boy by me. (√)。
  • 使用感官动词和使役动词的第五类句型，变成被动语态时要加上to
    • He was seen to enter the house by me.
    • My car was made to be washed by me.
  • We call this puppy Happy. (名词作补语，小狗的名字叫Happy。) → This puppy is called Happy by us.
  • This movie made us happy. (使役动词，形容词作补语。) → We were made to happy by this movie.
  • Mom made us study hard.(使役动词，动词原形作补语。) → We were made to study hard by Mom.
  • I had the box carried by the boy.(使役动词，过去分词作补语。) → The box was had to be carried by the boy by me. (×) → The box was made to be carried by the boy by me. (√)。
  • He was seen to enter the house by me.
  • My car was made to be washed by me.

    • I saw him enter the house. → He was seen to enter the house by me.
    • I made my car washed. → My car was made to be washed by me.

通过解题治疗语法7

  • 将下列句子改写成被动语态
    • I made her stay here. → She was made to stay here by me.
    • I saw her swim in the river. → She was seen to swim in the river by me.
    • I have three sisters. → 三个典型的不能变成被动语态的动词：have(拥有), resemble(和……像), become(适合)。
  • I made her stay here. → She was made to stay here by me.
  • I saw her swim in the river. → She was seen to swim in the river by me.
  • I have three sisters. → 三个典型的不能变成被动语态的动词：have(拥有), resemble(和……像), become(适合)。

三个典型的不能变成被动语态的动词

  • have(拥有), resemble(和……像), become(适合)不能变成被动语态
    • She has two daughters. → Two daughter are had by her. (×)。
    • He resembles his father. → His father is resembled by him. (×)。
    • This dress becomes you. → You are become by this dress. (×)。
  • She has two daughters. → Two daughter are had by her. (×)。
  • He resembles his father. → His father is resembled by him. (×)。
  • This dress becomes you. → You are become by this dress. (×)。
56使用by以外的介词构成被动语态 ›

共 3 个小节 · 11 条要点 · 65 句原句摘录

被动语态的构成及特殊情况

  • 被动语态的基本结构是“be + 过去分词 + by + 宾格”
    • be known by的意思是“通过……得知”，用于表示判断的标准。
  • 有十种特殊情况，be动词后接不同的介词
    • be surprised at 因……而吃惊。
    • be satisfied with 对……而满意。
    • be covered with 被……覆盖。
    • be filled with 充满……。
    • be interested in 对……感兴趣。
    • be made of 由……构成(物理变化)。
    • be made from 由……构成(化学变化)。
    • be known to被……所知。
    • be known as 作为……被知。
    • be known for 因……而有名。
  • be known by的意思是“通过……得知”，用于表示判断的标准。
  • be surprised at 因……而吃惊。
  • be satisfied with 对……而满意。
  • be covered with 被……覆盖。
  • be filled with 充满……。
  • be interested in 对……感兴趣。
  • be made of 由……构成(物理变化)。
  • be made from 由……构成(化学变化)。
  • be known to被……所知。
  • be known as 作为……被知。
  • be known for 因……而有名。

  • 翻译句子并填写恰当的介词

    • She is well known to neighbors.
    • I am interested in classical music.
    • He was surprised at the result.
    • This chair is made of metal.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • I was read a book by my mom.
    • He was made study harder by his dad.
    • The baby is taken care by her.
    • Wine is made of grapes.
    • She is resembled by her brother.

提高语法水平的处方

  • 将下列句子改写成被动语态
    • I saw her swim in the river.
    • The fire has damaged the building.
    • They will close the museum.
    • They are building the highest tower in the world.
    • My mom made the room cleaned.
    • They don’t like their English teacher.
    • She didn’t take care of the baby.
    • He didn’t break the window.
    • Did Mr. Smith build the hospital?
    • He will give me a book.
  • 选择恰当的答案
    • The machine (invented / was invented) in Canada.
    • I (wasn’t invited / didn’t invite) to the party.
    • I was (given / given to) a gift by Angela.
    • He was made (stay / to stay) at home all day.
    • She was seen (enter / to enter) her house.
    • My room was filled (by / with) books.
    • Jack is known (for / to) his honesty.
  • 找出错误并改正
    • The bike repairs by him.
    • She confused with his words.
    • Tom was injure in a car accident.
    • This house was build 100 years ago.
    • This chair is made by wood.
    • This jam is made by apples.
    • He didn’t satisfied with the result.
    • I was surprised with the news.
    • She was laughed at her friends.
    • He is well known as neighbors.
  • 选出变成被动语态后错误的一项
    • ① He made the doll for the girl.
    • ② I help many poor children.
    • ③ She wrote a letter.
    • ④ They will build the tallest hospital.
    • ⑤ We call him Jimmy.
  • 选出正确的句子
    • ① I’ve been interesting in dancing since I was a kid.
    • ② This frame was made to my sister.
    • My bicycle was stolen last year.
    • This bowl is made with glass.
    • Who was not choosing at the election?
  • 选出错误的一项
    • ① King Sejong invented Hangul.
    • ② The 17th World Cup was held in Korea.
    • The thief was caught by the policeman.
    • It was built in 1920.
    • Shakespeare was written Romeo and Juliet.
  • 选出正确的被动语态
    • They sent him to a hospital by taxi.
    • Where did she buy the hat?
